| es-estimated-tax-0004 / LF25 | ## Who this applies to | Ordinary common-territory IRPF taxpayers carrying on an economic activity calculate payments under [RIRPF arts. 109–112](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a109). | RIRPF art.109 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a109 | sources/RIRPF.reader.txt LF6232–6232: “obligados a autoliquidar e ingresar en el Tesoro, en concepto de pago a cuenta del Impuesto sobre la Renta de las Personas Físicas, la cantidad que resulte de lo establecido en los artículos siguientes, sin perjuicio de las excepciones previstas en los apartados siguientes.”; sources/RIRPF.reader.txt LF6234–6234: “70 por ciento de los ingresos de la actividad fueron objeto de retención o ingreso a cuenta.”; sources/RIRPF.reader.txt LF6240–6240: “inicio de la actividad se tendrá en cuenta el porcentaje de ingresos que hayan sido objeto de retención o ingreso a cuenta durante el período a que se refiere el pago fraccionado.”; sources/RIRPF.reader.txt LF6236–6236: “actividades agrícolas o ganaderas no estarán obligados a efectuar pago fraccionado en relación con las mismas si, en el año natural anterior, al menos el 70 por ciento de los ingresos procedentes de la explotación, con excepción de las subvenciones corrientes y de capital y”; sources/RIRPF.reader.txt LF6238–6238: “actividades forestales no estarán obligados a efectuar pago fraccionado en relación con las mismas si, en el año natural anterior, al menos el 70 por ciento de los ingresos procedentes de la actividad, con excepción de las subvenciones corrientes y de capital y de las” | 2026-10-28; Express law / published administrative method; stated conflicts and precise limits retained |
| es-estimated-tax-0005 / LF25 | ## Who this applies to | Direct assessment uses Modelo 130 | RIRPF art.109 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a109 | sources/RIRPF.reader.txt LF6232–6232: “obligados a autoliquidar e ingresar en el Tesoro, en concepto de pago a cuenta del Impuesto sobre la Renta de las Personas Físicas, la cantidad que resulte de lo establecido en los artículos siguientes, sin perjuicio de las excepciones previstas en los apartados siguientes.”; sources/RIRPF.reader.txt LF6234–6234: “70 por ciento de los ingresos de la actividad fueron objeto de retención o ingreso a cuenta.”; sources/RIRPF.reader.txt LF6240–6240: “inicio de la actividad se tendrá en cuenta el porcentaje de ingresos que hayan sido objeto de retención o ingreso a cuenta durante el período a que se refiere el pago fraccionado.”; sources/RIRPF.reader.txt LF6236–6236: “actividades agrícolas o ganaderas no estarán obligados a efectuar pago fraccionado en relación con las mismas si, en el año natural anterior, al menos el 70 por ciento de los ingresos procedentes de la explotación, con excepción de las subvenciones corrientes y de capital y”; sources/RIRPF.reader.txt LF6238–6238: “actividades forestales no estarán obligados a efectuar pago fraccionado en relación con las mismas si, en el año natural anterior, al menos el 70 por ciento de los ingresos procedentes de la actividad, con excepción de las subvenciones corrientes y de capital y de las” | 2026-10-28; Express law / published administrative method; stated conflicts and precise limits retained |

Cuando la cuantía de los rendimientos NETOS (*) de actividades económicas del obligado tributario, obtenidos en el ejercicio anterior al que corresponde el trimestre por el que se efectúa la autoliquidación, haya sido igual o inferior a 12.000 euros, se consignará en esta”; sources/I131.reader.txt LF217–217: “Casilla 10. Se anotará en esta casilla el resultado de efectuar la operación indicada en el formulario (Casilla 07 – Casilla 08 – Casilla 09). Se trasladará a esta casilla el resultado de restar al importe de la casilla 10, los importes consignados, en su caso, en las casillas 11 y 12. En el supuesto de autoliquidación complementaria, debemos consignar en esta casilla el resultado a ingresar de las autoliquidaciones, modelo 131, presentadas anteriormente por el mismo ejercicio y período. Se consignará en esta casilla el resultado de restar al importe reflejado en la casilla 13 el importe consignado, en su caso, en la casilla 14. | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF1 | ---<br>name: es-estimated-tax<br>description: Use this skill whenever asked about Spanish estimated income tax payments (pagos fraccionados) for self-employed individuals (autonomos). Trigger on phrases like "Modelo 130", "pagos fraccionados", "estimated tax Spain", "IRPF quarterly", "Spanish advance tax", "autonomo tax payments", "estimacion directa", "Modelo 131", or any question about quarterly income tax prepayment obligations under the IRPF. Covers the quarterly filing schedule (Apr 20, Jul 20, Oct 20, Jan 30), the 20% cumulative computation method, the 70% withholding exemption, penalties for late filing, and payment procedures. ALWAYS read this skill before touching any estimated tax work for Spain.<br>version: 2.0<br>jurisdiction: ES<br>tax_year: 2026<br>last_updated: 2026-09-23<br>review_status: pending_review<br>trust_label: Built by Michael Cutajar and the OpenAccountants team<br>drafted_by: OpenAccountants<br>approved_by: pending<br>depends_on:<br>  - income-tax-workflow-base<br>category: international<br>tier: 2<br>license: AGPL-3.0-or-later (code) / OpenAccountants Guide License v1.0 (content)<br>--- | Current supplied metadata/H1 and scope; old publication-year/version wording retained as history. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF21 | How a self-employed person in Spain pays income tax during the year: who must file modelo 130 in estimacion directa, who files modelo 131 under the module method, the percentage on each, the exemption for professionals whose invoices are withheld, the box by box computation, the quarterly deadlines, what happens when a quarter is late, and how the year's payments come back in the annual return. Figures are for tax year 2026. Two of the pages used carry their own dating and are named where used: the Agencia Tributaria page giving the 2026 late payment interest sits inside the Renta 2025 manual, and the Agencia Tributaria page on who the module method applies to states limits that the statute does not, which Section 5.6 prints side by side. | Current supplied metadata/H1 and scope; old publication-year/version wording retained as history. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF79 | - **R-ES-ET-1, estimacion objetiva (modulos).** Trigger: the client is on the module method. Message: "An activity on the module method pays on modelo 131, not modelo 130, and the percentage depends on the number of employees. Section 5.6 of this Guide covers modelo 131 in outline; the module amounts themselves are set by the annual module order and are not in this Guide." | Current Ask the client first and The base require activity/method/territory, prior and current receipts, withholding, previous boxes/payment evidence and housing tests. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF80 | - **R-ES-ET-2, cross-border income.** Trigger: the client is not resident in Spain, or has treaty income. Message: "A non-resident does not file modelo 130. Cross-border IRPF is outside this Guide." | Current Ask the client first and The base require activity/method/territory, prior and current receipts, withholding, previous boxes/payment evidence and housing tests. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF81 | - **R-ES-ET-3, an empresario claiming the exemption.** Trigger: a business activity, not a professional one, claims the withholding exemption. Message: "Article 109.2 Real Decreto 439/2007 gives the exemption to professional activities. Articles 109.3 and 109.4 give the same test to farming, livestock and forestry. A business activity in seccion primera of the IAE tariffs has no exemption." | Current Ask the client first and The base require activity/method/territory, prior and current receipts, withholding, previous boxes/payment evidence and housing tests. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF82 | - **R-ES-ET-4, a company.** Trigger: the client is an SL or another taxpayer of the corporate tax. Message: "A company pays its instalments on modelo 202 under the corporate tax, not on modelo 130. The prior year figure is net income from economic activities BEFORE the reduction for irregular income and the other reductions on net income. Someone who carried on no activity at all last year is treated as having nil, so they take the top amount. Where the same person files both forms in the same quarter, the reduction may be split between box 13 of modelo 130 and box 09 of modelo 131, and the two together may not exceed the amount for the quarter. And where the reduction is larger than what is left to pay, the excess carries to a later quarter of the same year whose positive amount allows it. | Current low-prior-income table and The base boxes13–15; quarterly100/75/50/25 with distinct prior-year/same-year tests. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF257 | Notes on the boxes that catch people. Box 04 takes the reduced percentage only for the Ceuta and Melilla credit under article 68.4 Ley 35/2006, and, for the third and fourth quarters of 2026 only, for the Isla de La Palma under disposicion adicional 57 of the same law as written by Real Decreto-Ley number 23 of 2026, of 8 September. Where the person also runs activities that do not qualify, the general percentage applies to those. Box 16 is only for a loan on a main home bought, or a refurbishment already being paid for, before 1 January 2013, it is not available at all where the money goes to building or extending the home, and the instructions rule it out entirely for someone who also files modelo 131, for someone running both farming and non-farming activities, and for someone who has told an employer about the same loan on modelo 145. A negative box 19 in the first three quarters is marked "A deducir" and used against a later quarter; a negative box 19 in the fourth quarter is marked "Negativa" and the position is settled in the annual return instead. Box 16 needs a positive box 14 and may never be more than box 14 less box 15. | Current Rates and The base statutory/calculation family; annual new-activity relief separately linked. |

Articles 109.3 and 109.4 give the same test to farming and livestock, and to forestry, measured on the income of the holding with grants and compensation left out. | Current Who this applies / per-activity70% prior-year/start-period receipt test. | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF263 | Four things the test is not. It is not tested on the current year: it looks back one calendar year, so the position can flip each January. It is not available to a business activity. It is not a partial relief: it removes the obligation for that activity, and it does not reduce anything. And in the year the activity starts there is no preceding year, so article 109.5 measures the share over the period the payment itself covers, which means the answer can differ quarter by quarter in the first year. | Current Who this applies / per-activity70% prior-year/start-period receipt test. Old80%-receipts example remains a qualifying example of this70% rule, not a new80% threshold. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF283 | An activity whose net income is fixed by the module method pays on modelo 131, on the same four dates, under its own [modelo 131 instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/impuestos-tasas/impuesto-sobre-renta-personas-fisicas/modelo-131-irpf______sionales-estimacion-objetiva-fraccionado_/instrucciones.html). Box 01 is the net income the modules produce from the data-base at 1 January, or at the start date where the activity was not carried on in the previous year, after the employment and investment incentives and the correcting indices, and after the general reduction on module net income that the annual module order sets for the year. The reduction for 2026 is in the table below. Box 02 applies the percentage that matches the number of employees, from the table in 5.1. Box 03 and box 04 are for an activity where no data-base can be fixed, worked on the quarter's sales. Box 05 and box 06 are the farming section. Box 07 adds them, box 08 takes off the quarter's withholding, box 09 is the reduction from the table in 5.2, and box 10 is box 07 less box 08 less box 09. Box 11 to box 15 then run as boxes 15 to 19 of modelo 130 do, with one difference: the home loan deduction in box 12 uses the lower percentage in the table below on box 01, the modelo 130 percentage on box 03, or the modelo 130 percentage on box 05 where only the farming section is filled, and its cap is a yearly one measured across all four returns of the year. For an activity qualifying for the Ceuta and Melilla credit, and exceptionally in the third and fourth quarters of 2026 for the Isla de La Palma, the instructions multiply the percentages by the factor zero point four rather than printing a separate rate. | Current Rates and The base statutory/calculation family; annual new-activity relief separately linked. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF315 | The two official sources disagree, and this Guide prints both. The higher limits come from disposicion transitoria 32 Ley 35/2006, whose heading still reads "en los ejercicios 2016 a 2024". Three Real Decreto-ley in turn tried to extend them and the Congress of Deputies left each one without effect, which is why the statute now prints the lower figures for 2026. The Agencia Tributaria's own page, updated in September 2026, still states the higher limits for 2016 to 2026 inclusive. Do not tell anyone they are inside or outside the module method on one of these sources: check the position with the Agencia Tributaria for the year in question, because the answer decides whether they file modelo 130 or modelo 131. | Current Rates and The base statutory/calculation family; annual new-activity relief separately linked. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF321 | Article 27 Ley 58/2003 governs a return filed late WITHOUT a prior demand from the tax office. It is not a fixed ladder of bands: it is one per cent, plus the same again for each complete month of delay, and a single higher figure once twelve months have passed. | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF354 | How the band is picked. At or below the base figure above the failure is minor. Above it, it is still minor where there was no concealment, and serious where there was. It is never minor where false invoices were used, where bad record keeping affected more than a tenth of the base, or where tax withheld from others was not paid over. Fraudulent means make it very serious in every case. | Current Penalties and interest: LGT27 voluntary surcharge,28 enforcement,191 conduct classification,198 nil-return rule and26 actual period interest. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF395 | ### 7.3 How a pago fraccionado meets the annual return, and the other forms | Presentation heading/table separator, not a separate substantive tax rule. Current Deadlines and filing / annualIRPF crediting, distinctVAT303/payer111 and entity/individual status. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF397 | The pago fraccionado is a payment on account of the person's own IRPF, not a separate tax and not an expense. In the annual return, modelo 100, the year's payments are set against the tax finally due together with the withholding on the certificates, and any excess comes back as a refund. This is the only route by which an overpaid year comes back: there is no refund inside the year, only a negative result carried to a later quarter. The annual computation itself is in the `es-income-tax` Guide. | Current Deadlines and filing / annualIRPF crediting, distinctVAT303/payer111 and entity/individual status. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF399 | Two neighbouring obligations run on the same quarterly calendar and are not this form. VAT is self-assessed on modelo 303: the mechanics are in the `es-vat-return` Guide, and the form's own [modelo 303 instructions for 2026](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/todas-gestiones/impuestos-tasas/iva/modelo-303-iva-autoliquidacion_/instrucciones-2026.html) carry the boxes. A company pays instalments of the corporate tax on modelo 202, on a different calendar and a different computation; the [modelo 202 instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/todas-gestiones/impuestos-tasas/impuesto-sobre-sociedades/modelo-202-is-i_____resencia-territorio-fraccionado_/instrucciones/Instrucciones-para-2025.html) on the sede are headed "Instrucciones periodo 2025 y siguientes", so the same page governs 2026 and there is no newer page to look for. Neither form is covered here. | Current Deadlines and filing / annualIRPF crediting, distinctVAT303/payer111 and entity/individual status. |

The only figure that goes into box 15 of a later quarter is a negative box 19, which arises when the reduction in box 13 is larger than box 12. | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF476 | 1. Fix the estimation method and the activity type. Estimacion directa goes on modelo 130, the module method on modelo 131, and which one is available turns on the limits in 5.6, on which two official sources disagree. [Ley 35/2006](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764) | Current The base / Modelo130 and131 ordered computation, with annualIRPF handoff. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF477 | 2. Test the exemption before doing any arithmetic. For a professional activity, article 109.2 asks whether at least the share in the table in 5.1 of LAST calendar year's activity income bore withholding; articles 109.3 and 109.4 ask the same of farming, livestock and forestry; article 109.5 covers a start year. If it is met, that activity files nothing. [Real Decreto 439/2007](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820) | Current The base / Modelo130 and131 ordered computation, with annualIRPF handoff. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF478 | 3. Build the base. For modelo 130, income less deductible expenses from 1 January to the last day of the quarter, under the rules of the estimacion directa modality in use; for the farming section, the quarter's gross income with capital grants and compensation excluded. [Real Decreto 439/2007](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820) | Current The base / Modelo130 and131 ordered computation, with annualIRPF handoff. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF479 | 4. Apply the percentage from the table in 5.1, using the reduced figure only for a Ceuta and Melilla activity or, in the third and fourth quarters of 2026, for the Isla de La Palma. [Modelo 130 instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/impuestos-tasas/impuesto-sobre-renta-personas-fisicas/modelo-130-irpf______esionales-estimacion-directa-fraccionado_/instrucciones.html) | Current The base / Modelo130 and131 ordered computation, with annualIRPF handoff. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF480 | 5. Deduct, in the order the form uses: earlier quarters' positive results, then the withholding article 110.3 allows for this activity, then the reduction in 5.2, then any unused negative result of an earlier quarter, then the home loan deduction if it is available. [Modelo 130 instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/impuestos-tasas/impuesto-sobre-renta-personas-fisicas/modelo-130-irpf______esionales-estimacion-directa-fraccionado_/instrucciones.html) | Current The base / Modelo130 and131 ordered computation, with annualIRPF handoff. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF481 | 6. File and pay between the 1st and the 20th of April, July and October, and between the 1st and the 30th of January for the fourth quarter, moving a non-working day on. File a negative return where nothing is due. [Pagos fraccionados, plazos](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/irpf/retenciones-ingresos-cuenta-pagos-fraccionados/pagos-fraccionados/plazos-declaracion-ingreso.html) | Current The base / Modelo130 and131 ordered computation, with annualIRPF handoff. |

| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF1 | ---<br>name: es-estimated-tax<br>description: Calculates 2026 Spanish quarterly IRPF advances for ordinary common-territory self-employed taxpayers. Covers Modelo 130 cumulative direct assessment, quarter-only agricultural receipts, activity-specific withholding exemptions, low-prior-income reductions, negative-result utilisation and the legacy home-loan adjustment. Explains Modelo 131 payment mechanics and the need for an activity-specific module worksheet, distinguishing the ordinary statutory baseline from AEAT's expressly preserved 2026 post-repeal entry and election route. Includes Ceuta and Melilla rate reductions and the Q3/Q4 La Palma extension, filing windows, annual crediting and sourced penalties. Use for pagos fraccionados, IRPF trimestral, Modelo 130, Modelo 131, autonomous-worker estimated tax, a 70% withholding test, quarterly losses or prior-quarter payments. Foral and non-resident computations remain separate.<br>version: 2.4<br>jurisdiction: ES<br>tax_year: 2026<br>last_updated: 2026-10-28<br>review_status: pending_review<br>trust_label: Built by Michael Cutajar and the OpenAccountants team<br>drafted_by: OpenAccountants<br>approved_by: pending<br>depends_on:<br>  - income-tax-workflow-base<br>category: international<br>tier: 2<br>license: AGPL-3.0-or-later (code) / OpenAccountants Guide License v1.0 (content)<br>--- | Current LF1, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF19 | # Spain: quarterly IRPF advances, 2026 | Current LF19, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF21 | **Sourced from the law.** Independent whole-pack review and accountant sign-off are pending. Calculate quarterly IRPF advances from the correct activity and period. An advance is a payment toward annual IRPF, not a deductible business expense. | Current LF21, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF23 | ## Who this applies to | Current LF23, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF25 | Ordinary common-territory IRPF taxpayers carrying on an economic activity calculate payments under [RIRPF arts. 109–112](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a109). Direct assessment uses Modelo 130; objective assessment uses Modelo 131. Establish the actual tax method, activity and residence first. Corporate taxpayers use the corporate instalment route; [Bizkaia, Álava, Gipuzkoa and Navarra personal-business instalments](es-foral-income-tax.md) use their separately supplied territorial methods and the stated payment boundaries. IRNR uses its own payment rules. A Spanish resident's foreign customer or foreign income does not by itself remove their resident IRPF obligations. | Current LF25, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF27 | Test the withholding exemption **per eligible activity before computing a payment**. Professionals qualify where at least 70% of the preceding calendar year's activity receipts bore withholding/payments on account. Agricultural/livestock and forestry activities have corresponding tests excluding current/capital subsidies and compensation from their denominator. Ordinary business activities do not inherit the professional exemption. In an activity's start year, use the qualifying withholding share for the payment period under art. 109.5 and the form instructions, rather than inventing a previous-year amount. An exempt activity has no return/payment obligation for that activity; another non-exempt activity can still require a return. The exemption ratio is the share of **receipts subject to withholding**, not tax withheld divided by turnover ([art. 109](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a109)). | Current LF27, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF29 | For an income-attribution entity, each partner/member makes their own payment in proportion to their profit share; do not file the individual's Modelo 130 in the entity's name ([art. 112](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a112)). | Current LF29, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF31 | ## Rates, bands and thresholds | Current LF31, exact original unit |

Being below a receipts limit alone does not establish entry: check eligible activity/IAE, specific activity magnitudes, renunciation, incompatibility and VAT/IGIC coordination under the Act and annual order. The annual order's art. 3 refers back to the statutory 2026 limits; it does not itself enact the higher thresholds ([Orden HAC/1425/2025 art. 3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2025-25272#a3)). | Current LF70, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF72 | The earlier25October read identified a real textual difference: current **LIRPF DT 32 ends the extension at2024**, after notes removing the non-convalidated extensions. That ordinary text is retained, but it is not the full 2026 administrative answer. AEAT's **1 April 2026 effects note expressly considers both RDL 16/2025 and RDL 2/2026 after non-convalidation and maintains the former higher limits for 2026**. Its current eligibility page supplies the 2026 amounts in the administrative column above. Apply that published 2026 route, retain its authority and the taxpayer's actual method history, and do not treat either repealed decree as live legislation. This corrects the prior instruction to use the lower figures as the operational answer. ([DT 32](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dttrigesimasegunda); [AEAT eligibility](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/empresarios-individuales-profesionales/contribuyentes-modulos/quien-se-aplica/irpf.html); [AEAT post-repeal explanation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/todas-noticias/2026/abril/1/nota-sobre-efectos-ambito-tributario-febrero.html).) | Current LF72, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF74 | For a full preceding year, an otherwise eligible trader with 200,000 total ordinary receipts,100,000 required-invoice B2B receipts and 220,000 relevant purchases passes the three 2026 numerical limits. Amounts exactly 250,000/125,000/250,000 pass; any applicable limb0.01 higher fails. A prior six-month activity with 100,000/50,000/110,000 annualises to 200,000/100,000/220,000 before comparison. If an identical/similar activity with common direction and shared means requires aggregating 60,000 additional receipts,260,000 fails the receipts test. These controls do not replace the annual-order activity computation or the other eligibility gates. ([AEAT current limits and aggregation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/empresarios-individuales-profesionales/contribuyentes-modulos/quien-se-aplica/irpf.html); [LIRPF 31](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a31).) | Current LF74, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF76 | The post-repeal note's IRPF section also confirms validity of renunciations/revocations submitted while the two decrees operated. Retain the actual filing and date: RDL 2/2026 art. 11 provided **5–16 February 2026** and recognised December 2025 submissions, plus January 2026 submissions made while RDL 16/2025 operated. A 30 January filing is not preserved by that latter limb alone. A 15 February submission meets the exceptional date test; 17 February is outside it and needs a different applicable route. For an established activity, RIRPF 33 recognises **timely filing of the first-quarter direct-assessment instalment declaration** as implicit renunciation; do not replace the filing act with proof that cash was collected. For a genuine new start, use its separate statutory route for the payment on account corresponding to the **first quarter of exercising that activity**, within the prescribed period. Renunciation normally lasts at least **three years** and continues unless revoked within the applicable window; exceeding the relevant preceding-year eligibility limit makes the renunciation treated as not presented. RIRPF 35 retains the same-year exception for existing activities when a new activity starts outside módulos or with a renunciation; it does not create an unrestricted later-return election. Continuing thresholds do not reopen a window or prove a particular return correction. ([RDL 2/2026 art. 11](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-2547#a1-3); [AEAT post-repeal note](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/todas-noticias/2026/abril/1/nota-sobre-efectos-ambito-tributario-febrero.html); [RIRPF33–36](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a33).) | Current LF76, exact original unit |

| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF99 | A positive result is payable. A negative final result in Q1–Q3 is recorded as **A deducir** for permitted later positive payments in the same year; Q4 uses the negative-return route. A cumulative activity loss already affects the next quarter's cumulative base; it is not also a box 15 credit. A negative box 19 created by the low-income reduction is different. Neither is an automatic quarterly cash refund. Preserve each earlier return's boxes, payment and unused-credit balance; generic AEAT bank wording or an April debit alone does not prove a Modelo 130 payment. | Current LF99, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF101 | ### Legacy home adjustment and the two official-text errors | Current LF101, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF103 | First verify entitlement under LIRPF DT 18, including the qualifying pre-2013 acquisition/rehabilitation history. It is not the national rental credit. Construction/extension payments do not qualify for this instalment adjustment. For direct assessment, apply the statutory below-33,007.20 forecast-gross-income test, annualising first-quarter receipts as prescribed. For a fixed module worksheet, test the prescribed module net amount; where no base data exist, use the specified forecast-sales test instead. The Modelo 131 instructions combine its fixed-module and no-data routes for the threshold. Farming uses the relevant forecast receipts excluding capital grants and compensation. Mixed farming/non-farming routes, concurrent 130/131 obligations and the specified employer housing communication prevent this adjustment ([RIRPF art. 110.3.d](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a110); [LIRPF DT 18](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#dtdecimoctava)). | Current LF103, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF105 | The current AEAT Modelo 130 web instructions wrongly append an annual 660.14 cap to the **non-farming** direct route and describe box 19's subtraction in reverse. The regulation and gazetted form resolve both: non-farming direct relief has a **660.14 per-quarter** ceiling, and box 19 is **17 minus 18**. The farming route's 660.14 cap is annual. Do not carry either web-text error into a calculation ([RIRPF art. 110.3.d](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a110); [Orden HAP/258/2015 annex I](https://www.boe.es/boe/dias/2015/02/19/pdfs/BOE-A-2015-1656.pdf)). | Current LF105, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF107 | ### Modelo 131 | Current LF107, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF109 | Obtain an activity-specific annual-order module worksheet; this Guide does not replace all IAE module values, employment/investment incentives, correction indices, seasonal rules or agricultural indices. Ordinary data are fixed at 1 January or commencement, using prior-year data where the regulation prescribes. Employee units use the instruction's hours-based measure to two decimals, not a headcount rounded down to zero. Seasonal/part-year cases need the annual order's specific daily/period computation. | Current LF109, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF111 | Box 01 aggregates the relevant module net income after applicable adjustments and the 2026 general reduction; box 02 sums each activity's applicable employee-band payment. Boxes 03/04 handle the no-data quarter-sales route, and 05/06 the agricultural/livestock/forestry quarter route. The specified qualifying young-farmer/agricultural-worker DA 6 route reduces that last payment by 25% only after its conditions are verified. Box 07 sums 02, 04 and 06; box 08 deducts eligible quarter withholding; box 09 allocates the low-prior-income reduction; box 10 is `07 − 08 − 09`. Box 11 uses eligible prior negative results up to positive box 10. Box 12 is the qualifying home adjustment, also limited by positive `10 − 11` and the remaining annual 660.14 ceiling. Boxes 13–15 then give `13 = 10 − 11 − 12`, less the prior same-period payable amount in box 14 for a qualifying supplementary return, producing `15 = 13 − 14` ([Modelo 131 instructions](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/impuestos-tasas/impuesto-sobre-renta-personas-fisicas/modelo-131-irpf______sionales-estimacion-objetiva-fraccionado_/instrucciones.html)). | Current LF111, exact original unit |

| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF124 | ### Payment route and evidence | Current LF124, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF126 | For a payable Modelo130, the current AEAT instructions offer direct debit using the **complete account IBAN**. A non-collaborating bank account in the **SEPA zone** uses the specified collection intermediation by a collaborating entity; being a SEPA account alone is not the entire procedure. In the payable/NRC route, the available options include **account charge, credit/debit card or Bizum**, with the last alternatives introduced from2025. Keep the actual NRC/payment confirmation with the filing receipt. | Current LF126, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF128 | The instructions also list **acknowledgement of debt with a deferral or offset request, payment by transfer, or entry in the tax current-account system**, each subject to its own actual eligibility/procedure. Selecting a debt-request route does not itself prove that the tax was paid or the request approved. The cash-at-an-authorised-bank option in the cited text expressly refers to a generated-paper **2025 predeclaration**. Verify the permitted route for the actual period rather than treating that dated instruction as a blanket2026paper authorisation. Sources: [actual Modelo130 instructions, payment section](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/impuestos-tasas/impuesto-sobre-renta-personas-fisicas/modelo-130-irpf______esionales-estimacion-directa-fraccionado_/instrucciones.html), which cite HAP/2194/2013 arts.4/6–9 and EHA/1658/2009 art.5bis for the non-collaborating SEPA-account route. | Current LF128, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF130 | **Ceuta 2026 base change.** [LIRPF DA64](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#da-18) raises simplified direct assessment's combined provisions/hard-to-justify allowance to **10%**, retaining the ordinary 2,000 cap and other conditions, for the specified Ceuta activity entitled to art. 68.4 relief. It also raises the general módulos net-yield reduction to **10%** for qualifying Ceuta activity. These are alternative assessment routes, not two deductions against the same profit. For instalments, apply the changes only to 2026 periods whose filing window begins **more than one month after 3 September 2026**. The ordinary October window began 1 October and fails this test; the first ordinary affected quarterly window is **January2027 for Q4 2026**. Keep this base change separate from the **60% reduction in payment percentages**, and do not extend Ceuta's enhancement to Melilla/La Palma. See [es-income-tax](es-income-tax.md#ceuta-and-melilla-scale-source-and-tax-credit-calculation) for the annual calculation. Source read **2026-10-27**. | Current LF130, exact original unit |

A non-resident's Spanish business/PE is dealt with under IRNR, not an assumed Modelo 130 computation. | frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF156 | Hypothetical established non-exempt activity, no territorial/home/low-income adjustment: year-to-date receipts **30,000**, allowable expenses **10,000**, eligible withholding **500**. Prior Q1 positive box 07 is **1,500**, with no box 16. Net income is **20,000**; 20% gives **4,000**. Deduct **1,500** and **500**, leaving **2,000 payable**. Prior-year net income exceeds the low-income reduction ceiling. Each input is a stated example fact. | Current LF156, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF158 | ```oa-check<br>name: Modelo 130 Q2 cumulative computation<br>inputs:<br>  receipts_ytd: 30000<br>  allowable_costs_ytd: 10000<br>  prior_positive_box07: 1500<br>  prior_box16: 0<br>  withholding_ytd: 500<br>steps:<br>  net_ytd: receipts_ytd - allowable_costs_ytd<br>  box04: max(0, net_ytd) * 0.20<br>  box05: prior_positive_box07 - prior_box16<br>  box07: box04 - box05 - withholding_ytd<br>  box12: max(0, box07)<br>  payable: box12<br>expected:<br>  net_ytd: 20000.00<br>  box04: 4000.00<br>  payable: 2000.00<br>``` | Current LF158, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF179 | ### A loss and the low-income reduction are different carry mechanisms | Current LF179, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF181 | Hypothetical non-exempt business, prior-year net activity income **8,500**, no withholding, home adjustment, other activity or supplementary return. Q1 cumulative net income is **−3,000**. Box 04 and box 12 are zero; the **100** low-prior-income reduction creates **−100** in the final result, available for later positive quarters of the same year. The loss itself is not a **3,000** carried tax credit. | Current LF181, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF183 | At Q2, cumulative net income has recovered to **1,500**. The ordinary payment is **300**, with no earlier positive box 07 to subtract. Deduct Q2's **100** reduction and then the earlier unused **100**, leaving **100 payable**. The Q1 business loss already affected the new cumulative income; deducting it again as a separate credit would count it twice. | Current LF183, exact original unit |
